with open("dimer_con_hibrido.pdb", "w") as f_out:
    # Leer cadena C (sin cambios) y cadena D (desplazada 3 Å)
    cC = [0, 0, 0]  # placeholder
    cD = [0, 0, 0]
    chainC_atoms = []
    chainD_atoms = []

    for line in open("dimer_CD_pH5.pdb"):
        if line.startswith("ATOM"):
            ch = line[21]
            x, y, z = float(line[30:38]), float(line[38:46]), float(line[46:54])
            if ch == "C":
                chainC_atoms.append((x, y, z))
                cC[0] += x; cC[1] += y; cC[2] += z
            elif ch == "D":
                chainD_atoms.append((x, y, z))
                cD[0] += x; cD[1] += y; cD[2] += z

    cC = [c/len(chainC_atoms) for c in cC]
    cD = [c/len(chainD_atoms) for c in cD]

    # Vector normal
    import math
    vec_CD = [cD[i] - cC[i] for i in range(3)]
    norm = math.sqrt(sum(v**2 for v in vec_CD))
    vec_normalizado = [v/norm for v in vec_CD]
    shift = 3.0

    # Escribir cadena C (sin cambios)
    for line in open("dimer_CD_pH5.pdb"):
        if line.startswith("ATOM") and line[21] == "C":
            f_out.write(line)
        elif line.startswith("TER") and line[21] == "C":
            f_out.write(line)

    # Escri(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set$
bir cadena D desplazada
    for line in open("dimer_CD_pH5.pdb"):
        if line.startswith("ATOM") and line[21] == "D":
            x, y, z = float(line[30:38]), float(line[38:46]), float(line[46:54])
            x_new = x + shift * vec_normalizado[0]
            y_new = y + shift * vec_normalizado[1]
            z_new = z + shift * vec_normalizado[2]
            f_out.write(line[:30] + "%8.3f%8.3f%8.3f" % (x_new, y_new, z_new) + line[54:])

            bfac = float(line[60:66])

            x_new = x + shift * vec_normalizado[0]

            z_new = z + shift * vec_normalizado[2]

# 4. DPFs
def dpf(fn,move,ligtypes,fld,tag):
    coords=[]
    for l in open(move):
        if l.startswith("HETATM"):
            t=l.split(); coords.append((float(t[6]),float(t[7]),float(t[8])))
    ax=sum(c[0] for c in coords)/len(coords); ay=sum(c[1] for c in coords)/len(coords); az=sum(c[2] for c in coords)/len(coords)
    with open(fn,"w") as f:
        f.write("autodock_parameter_version 4.2\nsmooth 0.5\nabout %.3f %.3(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set$
f %.3f\nligand_types %s\nfld %s\n"%(ax,ay,az,ligtypes,fld))
        for t in ligtypes.split(): f.write("map %s.%s.map\n"%(tag,t))
        f.write("elecmap %s.e.map\ndesolvmap %s.d.map\nmove %s\nintelec\n"%(tag,tag,move))
        f.write("ga_pop_size 150\nga_num_evals 2500000\nga_num_generations 27000\nga_elitism 1\nga_mutation_rate 0.02\nga_crossover_rate 0.8\nga_window_size 10\nset_ga\n")
        f.write("sw_max_its 300\nsw_max_succ 4\nsw_max_fail 4\nsw_rho 1.0\nsw_lb_rho 0.01\nset_sw1\nls_search_freq 0.06\nset_psw1\nga_run 10\nanalysis 1\n")

python3 << 'PYEOF'
import os
for mol in ["adenina","arginina","espermina","resveratrol","curcumina"]:
    coords=[]
    for l in open(mol+".pdbqt"):
        if l.startswith(("ATOM","HETATM")):
            t=l.split(); coords.append((float(t[6]),float(t[7]),float(t[8])))
    ax=sum(c[0] for c in coords)/len(coords); ay=sum(c[1] for c in coords)/len(coords); az=sum(c[2] for c in coords)/len(coords)
    # detectar tipos del ligando
    types=set()
    for l in open(mol+".pdbqt"):
        if l.startswith(("ATOM","HETATM")): types.add(l.split()[-1])
    with open(mol+"_dimer.dpf","w") as f:
        f.write("autodock_parameter_version 4.2\nsmooth 0.5\nabout %.3f %.3f %.3f\nligand_types %s\nfld dimer_interface.maps.fld\n"%(ax,ay,az,"".join(sorted(types))))
        for t in sorted(types): f.write("map dimer_interface.%s.map\n"%t)
        f.write("elecmap dimer_interface.e.map\ndesolvmap dimer_interface.d.map\nmove %s.pdbqt\nintelec\n"%mol)
        f.write("ga_pop_size 150\nga_num_evals 2500000\nga_num_generations 27000\nga_elitism 1\nga_mutation_rate 0.02\nga_crossover_rate 0.8\nga_window_size 10\nset_ga\n")
        f.write("sw_max_its 300\nsw_max_succ 4\nsw_max_fail 4\nsw_rho 1.0\nsw_lb_rho 0.01\nset_sw1\nls_search_freq 0.06\nset_psw1\nga_run 10\nanalysis 1\n")
print("OK 5 dpfs")
PYEOF

python3 << 'PYEOF'
cx,cy,cz=[float(x) for x in open("_dimer_interface_center.txt").read().split()]

# Tipos del receptor
rtypes=set()
for line in open("dimer_CD_pH5.pdbqt"):
    if line.startswith("ATOM"): rtypes.add(line[77:79].strip())

# Tipos de ligando que necesitamos (todos los posibles)
ltypes={"C","HD","N","NA","OA","A","Zn"}

with open("dimer_interface_full.gpf","w") as f:
    f.write("npts 106 106 106\nspacing 0.375\ngridfld dimer_interface_full.maps.fld\n")
    f.write("receptor_types %s\nreceptor dimer_CD_pH5.pdbqt\n"%" ".join(sorted(rtypes)))
    f.write("gridcenter %.3f %.3f %.3f\nsmooth 0.5\ndielectric -0.1465\n"%(cx,cy,cz))

    for t in sorted(ltypes): f.write("map dimer_interface_full.%s.map\n"%t)
    f.write("elecmap dimer_interface_full.e.map\ndsolvmap dimer_interface_full.d.map\n")

import math

# 1. Extraer deca del híbrido existente
deca_lines = []
with open("hybrid_deca_quercetin.pdbqt") as f:
    count = 0
    for line in f:
        if line.startswith("HETATM") and " DVT " in line:
            deca_lines.append(line)
            count += 1
            if count >= 38: break

# Centroide del deca
deca_coords = []
for line in deca_lines:
    t = line.split()
    deca_coords.append((float(t[6]), float(t[7]), float(t[8])))
deca_center = [sum(c[i] for c in deca_coords)/len(deca_coords) for i in range(3)]

print(f"Deca: {len(deca_lines)} atomos, centroide = ({deca_center[0]:.2f}, {deca_center[1]:.2f}, {deca_center[2]:.2f})")

# 2. Para cada candidato, construir híbrido
for mol in ["berberina", "piperina", "curcumina"]:
    # Leer molécula
    mol_lines = []
    mol_coords = []
    with open(mol + ".pdbqt") as f:
        for line in f:
            if line.startswith(("ATOM", "HETATM")):
                mol_lines.append(line)
                t = line.split()
                mol_coords.append((float(t[6]), float(t[7]), float(t[8])))

    # Centroide de la molécula
    mol_center = [sum(c[i] for c in mol_(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set$
coords)/len(mol_coords) for i in range(3)]

    # Vector de molécula a deca
    vec = [deca_center[i] - mol_center[i] for i in range(3)]
    dist = math.dist(deca_center, mol_center)

    # Mover la molécula para que quede a ~3.5 Å del deca
    target_dist = 3.5
    shift = [vec[i] * (dist - target_dist) / dist for i in range(3)]

    # Verificar distancia mínima átomo-átomo
    min_dist = 999
    for d_line in deca_lines:

_pdbqt_fixed(fn, resn_filter=None):
    """Lee PDBQT por COLUMNAS FIJAS (robusto a formato de obabel)."""
    atoms = []
    for line in open(fn):
        if line.startswith(("ATOM","HETATM")):
            if resn_filter and line[17:20].strip() != resn_filter: continue
            name = line[12:16].strip()
            resn = line[17:20].strip()
            x = float(line[30:38]); y = float(line[38:46]); z = float(line[46:54])
            # tipo/elemento: ultima columna no-vacia
            toks = line.split()
            typ = toks[-1]
            atoms.append({"name":name,"resn":resn,"x":x,"y":y,"z":z,"typ":typ,"line":line})
    return atoms

# 1. Deca (columnas fijas, ya estaba bien)
deca = read_pdbqt_fixed("hybrid_deca_quercetin.pdbqt", "DVT")
deca_center = [sum(a[i] for a in deca for i in []) or 0,0,0]  # placeholder
dc = [sum(a[k] for a in deca)/len(deca) for k in ("x","y","z")]
print("Deca: %d atomos, centroide (%.2f,%.2f,%.2f)"%(len(deca),dc[0],dc[1],dc[2]))

# 2. Reconstruir cada hibrido con parsing por columnas
for mol, resn in [("berberina","BER"),("piperina","PIP"),("curcumina","CUR")]:
    m = read_pdbqt_fixed(mol+".pdbqt")
    mc = [sum(a[k] for a in m)/len(m) for k in ("x","y","z")]

    # vector mol->deca, acercar a 3.5 A
    vec = [dc[i]-mc[i] for i in range(3)]
    dist = math.dist(dc, mc)
    shift = [vec[i]*(dist-3.5)/dist for i in range(3)]
    for a in m:
        a["x"]+=shift[0]; a["y"]+=shift[1]; a["z"]+=shift[2]

    # distancia minima deca-mol (verifica sin clash)
    mind = min(math.dist((d["x"],d["y"],d["z"]),(a["x"],a["y"],a["z"])) for d in deca for a in m)

    # diametro de la molecula (verifica NO colapsada)
    diam = max(math.dist((a["x"],a["y"],a["z"]),(b["x"],b["y"],b["z"])) for i,a in enumerate(m) for b in m[i+1:](pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set$
)

    # PDBQT hibrido
    with open("hybrid_deca_%s.pdbqt"%mol,"w") as f:
        f.write("REMARK Hibrido deca + %s\nROOT\n"%mol)
        s=0
        for a in deca:
            s+=1
            f.write("HETATM%5d %-4s DVT A   1    %8.3f%8.3f%8.3f  1.00  0.00    %+6.3f %-2s\n"%(s,a["name"],a["x"],a["y"],a["z"], 1.3 if a["typ"]=="Zn" else -0.679, a["typ"]))
        for a in m:
            s+=1
            f.write("HETATM%5d %-4s %s A   2    %8.3f%8.3f%8.3f  1.00  0.00    %+6.3f %-2s\n"%(s,a["name"],resn,a["x"],a["y"],a["z"],0.0,a["typ"]))
        f.write("ENDROOT\nTORSDOF 0\n")

deca = read_fixed("hybrid_deca_quercetin.pdbqt","DVT")

for mol,resn in [("berberina","BER"),("piperina","PIP"),("curcumina","CUR")]:
    m = read_fixed(mol+".pdbqt")

    # Vector deca->mol (alejar en esa direccion hasta sin clash)
    dc=[sum(a[k] for a in deca)/len(deca) for k in "xyz"]
    mc=[sum(a[k] for a in m)/len(m) for k in "xyz"]
    vec=[mc[i]-dc[i] for i in range(3)]
    n=math.dist(mc,dc); vec=[v/n for v in vec]

    def min_dist(off):
        return min(math.dist((d["x"],d["y"],d["z"]),
                             (a["x"]+vec[0]*off,a["y"]+vec[1]*off,a["z"]+vec[2]*off))
                   for d in deca for a in m)

    # Buscar offset que de min_dist ~3.2 A
    off=0.0
    while min_dist(off)<3.2 and off<40: off+=0.5
    for a in m:
        a["x"]+=vec[0]*off; a["y"]+=vec[1]*off; a["z"]+=vec[2]*off
    md=min_dist(0.0)

    # PDB con CONECT (deca V-O y molécula por distancia)
    with open("hybrid_deca_%s.pdb"%mol,"w") as f:
        f.write("REMARK Hibrido deca + %s (sin clash, CONECT)\n"%mol)
        s=0; idx_deca=[]; idx_mol=[]
        for a in deca:
            s+=1; idx_deca.append(s)
            el="V" if a["typ"]=="Zn" else "O"
            f.write("HETATM%5d %-4s DVT A   1    %8.3f%8.3f%8.3f  1.00  0.00          %2s\n"%(s,a["name"],a["x"],a["y"],a["z"],el))
        for a in m:
            s+=1; idx_mol.append(s)
            el=a["typ"][0]
            f.write("HETATM%5d %-4s %s A   2    %8.3f%8.3f%8.3f  1.00  0.00          %2s\n"%(s,a["name"],resn,a["x"],a["y"],a["z"],el))
        # CONECT deca (V-O < 2.1)
        for i,a in enumerate(deca):
            part=[idx_deca[j] for j,b in enumerate(deca) if i!=j and math.dist((a["x"],a["y"],a["z"]),(b["x"],b["y"],b["z"]))<2.1]
            if part: f.write("CONECT%5d%s\n"%(idx_deca[i],"".join("%5d"%p for p in part)))
        # CONECT molecula (heavy < 1.9)
        for i,a in enumerate(m):
            part=[idx_mol[j] for j,b in enumerate(m) if i!=j and math.dist((a["x"],a["y"],a["z"]),(b["x"],b["y"],b["z"]))<1.9]
            if part: f.write("CON(pqr311) me@TerminalXolotl:/mnt/c/Users/Me/Desktop/CANIFARMA/br_set$
ECT%5d%s\n"%(idx_mol[i],"".join("%5d"%p for p in part)))
        f.write("END\n")

    # PDBQT (para docking) mismo posicionamiento
    with open("hybrid_deca_%s.pdbqt"%mol,"w") as f:
        f.write("REMARK Hibrido deca + %s\nROOT\n"%mol)
        s=0
        for a in deca:
            s+=1
            f.write("HETATM%5d %-4s DVT A   1    %8.3f%8.3f%8.3f  1.00  0.00    %+6.3f %-2s\n"%(s,a["name"],a["x"],a["y"],a["z"],1.3 if a["typ"]=="Zn" else -0.679,a["typ"]))
        for a in m:
            s+=1

for mol in ["berberina","piperina","curcumina"]:
    coords=[]; types=set()
    for l in open("hybrid_deca_%s.pdbqt"%mol):
        if l.startswith("HETATM"):
            t=l.split()
            coords.append((float(t[6]),float(t[7]),float(t[8])))
            types.add(t[-1])
    ax=sum(c[0] for c in coords)/len(coords)
    ay=sum(c[1] for c in coords)/len(coords)
    az=sum(c[2] for c in coords)/len(coords)
    with open("hybrid_deca_%s_dimer.dpf"%mol,"w") as f:
        f.write("autodock_parameter_version 4.2\nsmooth 0.5\n")
        f.write("about %.3f %.3f %.3f\n"%(ax,ay,az))
        f.write("ligand_types %s\nfld dimer_interface_full.maps.fld\n"%" ".join(sorted(types)))
        for t in sorted(types): f.write("map dimer_interface_full.%s.map\n"%t)
        f.write("elecmap dimer_interface_full.e.map\ndesolvmap dimer_interface_full.d.map\n")
        f.write("move hybrid_deca_%s.pdbqt\nintelec\n"%mol)
        f.write("ga_pop_size 150\nga_num_evals 2500000\nga_num_generations 27000\n")
        f.write("ga_elitism 1\nga_mutation_rate 0.02\nga_crossover_rate 0.8\nga_window_size 10\nset_ga\n")
        f.write("sw_max_its 300\nsw_max_succ 4\nsw_max_fail 4\nsw_rho 1.0\nsw_lb_rho 0.01\nset_sw1\n")
        f.write("ls_search_freq 0.06\nset_psw1\nga_run 10\nanalysis 1\n")
    print("OK hybrid_deca_%s_dimer.dpf (tipos: %s)"%(mol, sorted(types)))

# === A. Extraer poses de los 3 hibridos a PDB (con CONECT) ===
def extract(fn,out,org):
    atoms=[]; inm=False
    for line in open(fn):
        if line.startswith("DOCKED: MODEL"): inm=True
        elif line.startswith("DOCKED: ENDMDL"): break
        elif inm and line.startswith("DOCKED: ATOM"):
            t=line.split(); atoms.append((t[3],float(t[7]),float(t[8]),float(t[9])))
    with open(out,"w") as f:
        for i,(el,x,y,z) in enumerate(atoms):
            rn="DVT" if i<38 else org
            f.write("HETATM%5d %-4s %s A   1    %8.3f%8.3f%8.3f  1.00  0.00          %2s\n"%(i+1,el,rn,x,y,z,el[0]))
        for i,(e1,x1,y1,z1) in enumerate(atoms):
            part=[]
            for j,(e2,x2,y2,z2) in enumerate(atoms):
                if i==j: continue
                d=math.dist((x1,y1,z1),(x2,y2,z2))
                if i<38 and j<38 and d<2.1: part.append(j+1)
                if i>=38 and j>=38 and d<1.9: part.append(j+1)
            if part: f.write("CONECT%5d%s\n"%(i+1,"".join("%5d"%p for p in part)))
        f.write("END\n")
    print("OK %s (%d atomos)"%(out,len(atoms)))

extract("dlg_hybrid_deca_berberina_dimer.dlg","pose_ber.pdb","BER")
extract("dlg_hybrid_deca_piperina_dimer.dlg","pose_pip.pdb","PIP")
extract("dlg_hybrid_deca_curcumina_dimer.dlg","pose_cur.pdb","CUR")

# === B. Quercetin: choca con cadena D? (test de cuna) ===
chainD=[]
for line in open("dimer_CD_pH5.pdb"):
    if line.startswith("ATOM") and line[21]=="D":
        chainD.append((float(line[30:38]),float(line[38:46]),float(line[46:54])))
que=[]; inm=False
for line in open("dlg_que_closed.dlg"):
    if line.startswith("DOCKED: MODEL"): inm=True
    elif line.startswith("DOCKED: ENDMDL"): break
    elif inm and line.startswith("DOCKED: ATOM"):
        t=line.split(); que.append((float(t[7]),float(t[8]),float(t[9])))
dmin=min(math.dist(q,d) for q in que for d in chainD)
ncl=sum(1 for q in que for d in chainD if math.dist(q,d)<3.0)
print("\nQUERCETIN vs cadena D: dmin=%.2f A, clashes(<3A)=%d"%(dmin,ncl))
print("  (DeQue fue: dmin=2.22 A, clashes=7)")
if ncl==0: print("  => el quercetin ENCAJA sin tocar D: une SIN romper el dimero")
else:      print("  => el quercetin TAMBIEN choca con D: actuaria como cuna")
PYEOF
